- **tmux-worker**: Start Claude Code workers in tmux windows and get their replies back: start a worker in a worktree or folder, wait for its turn to end, send follow-ups, read its screen, interrupt, stop, and resume it. Do not use for Orca (orchestration, orca-cli), Paseo (paseo), or pi (pi-subagent), and do not use for ordinary subagent or parallel work, which uses built-in subagents.
